import cv2, numpy as np, os
out='C:/Users/scott/AppData/Local/Temp/opencode/stage1b/masks'; os.makedirs(out,exist_ok=True)
imgs={'la1_0':'C:/Users/scott/AppData/Local/Temp/opencode/stage1b/frames/notus_a1_0.jpg','la1_7570':'C:/Users/scott/AppData/Local/Temp/opencode/stage1b/frames/notus_a1_7570.jpg','ad_0':'C:/Users/scott/AppData/Local/Temp/opencode/stage1b/frames/adrian_0.jpg'}
for name,p in imgs.items():
    im=cv2.imread(p); hsv=cv2.cvtColor(im,cv2.COLOR_BGR2HSV)
    masks={}
    masks['red']=cv2.inRange(hsv,np.array([0,80,50]),np.array([10,255,255]))|cv2.inRange(hsv,np.array([170,80,50]),np.array([180,255,255]))
    masks['blue']=cv2.inRange(hsv,np.array([95,80,40]),np.array([135,255,255]))
    masks['white']=cv2.inRange(hsv,np.array([0,0,160]),np.array([180,45,255]))
    for k,m in masks.items():
        cv2.imwrite(os.path.join(out,f'{name}_{k}.png'),m)
print('done')

**Acceptance:** met (60 overlays, per-frame JSON, summaries, report, no existing file changed).

**Result (Proven, counted from the per-frame JSON):** frames with at least 4 keypoints at conf >= 0.3:
- Adrian 16 of 20 (4 with zero points)
- Notus A1 8 of 20 (12 with zero)
- Notus A 11 of 20 (7 with zero)

Indices 0-5 and 10 almost never appear. 8/16, 9/17, and 14/15 duplicate each other.

**By eye** (vetter_sheet_best_median_zero.png: best, median, and a zero-point frame per game): the zero-point frames show the lane, free-throw circle, arcs, and center line clearly. The model, not the video, is the problem. Detected points are sparse, and some sit off the painted lines.

**Conclusion:** models/court_keypoint_detector.pt is not reliable enough on these gyms to rebuild the homography every frame. Do not build Stage 2 on it alone.

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Hoop detection via court line detection.
1. Detect court lines (white lines on brown court)
2. Find the baseline (long horizontal line near the basket)
3. Compute rim position from court geometry
4. Track ball through rim region for shot detection

Court geometry (NBA/NCAA):
- Court: 94ft x 50ft
- Baseline to backboard: 4ft
- Backboard to rim center: ~1.5ft (rim extends 1.5ft from backboard)
- Rim height: 10ft
- Free throw line: 15ft from backboard (19ft from baseline)
- 3PT line: 22ft from basket center (corner)
"""

import cv2
import numpy as np
import csv
import os

VIDEO_PATH = "uploads/Liberty_Vs_Riverstone_Q1.webm"
OUT_CSV = "hoop_from_court.csv"
SHOT_CSV = "shots_v8.csv"
OUT_DIR = "v8_vis"
os.makedirs(OUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)

STRIDE = 2

# Court physical dimensions (ft)
COURT_W = 50.0
COURT_H = 94.0
BASELINE_TO_BACKBOARD = 4.0
BACKBOARD_TO_RIM = 1.5  # rim extends 1.5ft from backboard face
RIM_HEIGHT = 10.0
FT_LINE_FROM_BASELINE = 19.0
THREE_PT_CORNER_DIST = 22.0  # from basket center

def detect_court_lines(frame):
    """
    Detect white court lines on the brown court.
    Returns list of line segments [(x1,y1,x2,y2), ...]
    """
    gray = cv2.cvtColor(frame, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)
    h, w = frame.shape[:2]
    
    # Court lines are bright white on brown
    # Use adaptive thresholding since lighting varies
    blurred = cv2.GaussianBlur(gray, (5, 5), 0)
    
    # Detect bright lines
    _, bright = cv2.threshold(blurred, 0, 255, cv2.THRESH_BINARY + cv2.THRESH_OTSU)
    
    # Edge detection
    edges = cv2.Canny(blurred, 50, 150)
    
    # Combine: edges that are also bright
    line_mask = cv2.bitwise_and(edges, bright)
    
    # Morphological: connect broken line segments
    kernel_h = np.ones((1, 15), np.uint8)  # horizontal connector
    kernel_v = np.ones((15, 1), np.uint8)  # vertical connector
    line_mask = cv2.morphologyEx(line_mask, cv2.MORPH_CLOSE, kernel_h, iterations=2)
    line_mask = cv2.morphologyEx(line_mask, cv2.MORPH_CLOSE, kernel_v, iterations=1)
    
    # Hough line detection
    lines = cv2.HoughLinesP(line_mask, rho=1, theta=np.pi/180, 
                             threshold=50, minLineLength=w//8, maxLineGap=20)
    
    if lines is None:
        return []
    
    result = []
    for line in lines:
        x1, y1, x2, y2 = line[0]
        length = np.sqrt((x2-x1)**2 + (y2-y1)**2)
        angle = np.degrees(np.arctan2(y2-y1, x2-x1)) % 180
        
        # Filter: court lines are either horizontal (baseline, FT line) or vertical (sidelines)
        is_horizontal = abs(angle) < 15 or abs(angle - 180) < 15
        is_vertical = abs(angle - 90) < 15
        
        if length > w // 10 and (is_horizontal or is_vertical):
            result.append((x1, y1, x2, y2, angle, length))
    
    return result

def find_baseline_and_hoop(lines, frame_shape):
    """
    From detected court lines, find the baseline and compute hoop position.
    The baseline is the long horizontal line closest to the basket area.
    """
    h, w = frame_shape[:2]
    
    if not lines:
        return None
    
    # Separate horizontal and vertical lines
    h_lines = [(x1,y1,x2,y2,length) for x1,y1,x2,y2,angle,length in lines 
               if abs(angle) < 15 or abs(angle-180) < 15]
    v_lines = [(x1,y1,x2,y2,length) for x1,y1,x2,y2,angle,length in lines 
               if abs(angle-90) < 15]
    
    if not h_lines:
        return None
    
    # The baseline is typically the longest horizontal line in the lower portion
    # But with ceiling camera, it could be anywhere
    # Sort by length
    h_lines.sort(key=lambda x: -x[4])
    
    # Take the top 3 longest horizontal lines
    candidates = h_lines[:3]
    
    # The baseline is the one closest to the bottom of the frame (in most camera angles)
    # But with ceiling mount, we need to be smarter
    # Use the longest horizontal line as baseline
    bl = candidates[0]
    bx1, by1, bx2, by2, blength = bl
    
    # Baseline center
    baseline_cx = (bx1 + bx2) / 2
    baseline_cy = (by1 + by2) / 2
    
    # Determine which side of the court this baseline is on
    # The hoop is INWARD from the baseline (toward center court)
    # If baseline is in the lower half of frame, hoop is above it
    # If baseline is in the upper half, hoop is below it
    
    if baseline_cy > h * 0.5:
        # Baseline in lower half → hoop is above (toward top of frame)
        hoop_direction = -1
    else:
        # Baseline in upper half → hoop is below
        hoop_direction = 1
    
    # Find the sideline (vertical line) closest to the baseline center
    # This tells us which basket (left or right side)
    nearest_v = None
    nearest_v_dist = float('inf')
    for vx1, vy1, vx2, vy2, vlength in v_lines:
        v_cx = (vx1 + vx2) / 2
        dist = abs(v_cx - baseline_cx)
        if dist < nearest_v_dist:
            nearest_v_dist = dist
            nearest_v = (vx1, vy1, vx2, vy2)
    
    # Estimate hoop position
    # From baseline, the hoop is ~5.5ft inward (4ft to backboard + 1.5ft to rim)
    # In pixel terms, we need to estimate the scale
    # Court width is 50ft, which spans roughly the frame width
    ft_per_px = COURT_W / w
    
    hoop_offset_px = (BASELINE_TO_BACKBOARD + BACKBOARD_TO_RIM) / ft_per_px
    
    hoop_x = baseline_cx
    hoop_y = baseline_cy + hoop_direction * hoop_offset_px
    
    # If we have a sideline, the hoop is between the sideline and center
    if nearest_v:
        v_cx = (nearest_v[0] + nearest_v[2]) / 2
        # The basket is roughly 1/4 court width from the sideline
        court_quarter = w / 4
        if abs(baseline_cx - v_cx) < court_quarter:
            # This baseline is near a sideline → hoop is toward center from baseline
            if baseline_cx < v_cx:
                hoop_x = baseline_cx + court_quarter
            else:
                hoop_x = baseline_cx - court_quarter
    
    # Estimate rim radius in pixels
    # Rim diameter = 18 inches = 1.5ft
    rim_radius_px = (1.5 / 2) / ft_per_px
    
    return {
        'baseline': (bx1, by1, bx2, by2),
        'hoop_x': int(hoop_x),
        'hoop_y': int(hoop_y),
        'rim_radius': int(rim_radius_px),
        'ft_per_px': ft_per_px,
        'sideline': nearest_v,
        'h_lines': h_lines[:5],
        'v_lines': v_lines[:5]
    }

def detect_ball_near_hoop(frame, hoop_info):
    """Detect basketball near the computed hoop position."""
    hx, hy = hoop_info['hoop_x'], hoop_info['hoop_y']
    hr = hoop_info['rim_radius']
    h, w = frame.shape[:2]
    
    # ROI around hoop
    margin = int(hr * 3)
    x1 = max(0, hx - margin)
    y1 = max(0, hy - margin)
    x2 = min(w, hx + margin)
    y2 = min(h, hy + margin)
    
    roi = frame[y1:y2, x1:x2]
    if roi.size == 0:
        return None
    
    hsv = cv2.cvtColor(roi, cv2.COLOR_BGR2HSV)
    mask = cv2.inRange(hsv, np.array([8, 80, 80]), np.array([30, 255, 255]))
    mask = cv2.morphologyEx(mask, cv2.MORPH_OPEN, np.ones((3,3), np.uint8))
    
    contours, _ = cv2.findContours(mask, cv2.RETR_EXTERNAL, cv2.CHAIN_APPROX_SIMPLE)
    
    best = None
    best_score = -1
    
    for c in contours:
        a = cv2.contourArea(c)
        if a < 8 or a > 1000: continue
        (bx, by), br = cv2.minEnclosingCircle(c)
        bd = 2 * br
        if not (5 <= bd <= 40): continue
        
        full_bx = bx + x1
        full_by = by + y1
        dist = np.sqrt((full_bx - hx)**2 + (full_by - hy)**2)
        
        dist_score = max(0, 1.0 - dist / (hr * 2))
        circ = a / (np.pi * br * br) if br > 0 else 0
        score = 0.5 * dist_score + 0.3 * circ + 0.2 * min(1, a / 50)
        
        if score > best_score:
            best_score = score
            best = {'cx': full_bx, 'cy': full_by, 'diam': bd,
                    'dist_from_hoop': dist, 'score': score,
                    'in_hoop': dist < hr}
    
    return best

def detect_net_motion(frame_prev, frame_curr, hoop_info):
    """Detect motion in net region below the rim."""
    if frame_prev is None or frame_curr is None:
        return 0.0

#!/usr/bin/env python3
import cv2
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import sys
import os

def detect_hoop_and_lines(frame):
    gray = cv2.cvtColor(frame, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)
    blur = cv2.GaussianBlur(gray, (5,5), 0)
    # Hoop detection via HoughCircles
    circles = cv2.HoughCircles(blur, cv2.HOUGH_GRADIENT, dp=1.2, minDist=30,
                               param1=50, param2=30, minRadius=20, maxRadius=150)
    hoop_center = None
    hoop_radius = None
    if circles is not None:
        circles = np.uint16(np.around(circles))
        # Choose the circle with highest accumulator? just take first
        x, y, r = circles[0][0]
        hoop_center = (float(x), float(y))
        hoop_radius = float(r)
    # Line detection via HoughLinesP
    edges = cv2.Canny(blur, 50, 150, apertureSize=3)
    lines = cv2.HoughLinesP(edges, rho=1, theta=np.pi/180, threshold=80,
                            minLineLength=30, maxLineGap=10)
    # Separate vertical and horizontal lines based on angle
    vert_lines = []
    horiz_lines = []
    if lines is not None:
        for line in lines[:,0]:
            x1,y1,x2,y2 = line
            angle = np.abs(np.arctan2(y2-y1, x2-x1)) * 180 / np.pi
            if angle < 20 or angle > 160:  # near horizontal
                horiz_lines.append(((x1,y1),(x2,y2)))
            elif 70 < angle < 110:  # near vertical
                vert_lines.append(((x1,y1),(x2,y2)))
    # Compute average x for leftmost and rightmost vertical lines
    left_x = None
    right_x = None
    if vert_lines:
        # compute average x of each line
        line_xs = []
        for (p1,p2) in vert_lines:
            xs = [p1[0], p2[0]]
            line_xs.append(np.mean(xs))
        # sort
        sorted_idx = np.argsort(line_xs)
        left_x = np.mean([vert_lines[sorted_idx[0]][0][0], vert_lines[sorted_idx[0]][1][0]])
        right_x = np.mean([vert_lines[sorted_idx[-1]][0][0], vert_lines[sorted_idx[-1]][1][0]])
    # Compute average y for lowest and second lowest horizontal lines (baseline lowest y?)
    baseline_y = None
    ft_y = None
    if horiz_lines:
        line_ys = []
        for (p1,p2) in horiz_lines:
            ys = [p1[1], p2[1]]
            line_ys.append(np.mean(ys))
        sorted_idx = np.argsort(line_ys)
        # lowest y (smallest) is top of image? Since camera looks down, baseline likely higher y? We'll just take two extremes.
        baseline_y = np.mean([horiz_lines[sorted_idx[0]][0][1], horiz_lines[sorted_idx[0]][1][1]])
        ft_y = np.mean([horiz_lines[sorted_idx[-1]][0][1], horiz_lines[sorted_idx[-1]][1][1]])
    return hoop_center, hoop_radius, left_x, right_x, baseline_y, ft_y

def main(video_path, out_npy, stride=4):
    cap = cv2.VideoCapture(video_path)
    if not cap.isOpened():
        raise RuntimeError(f'Cannot open video {video_path}')
    fps = cap.get(5)
    frame_count = int(cap.get(7))
    Hs = []
    frame_indices = []
    frame_idx = 0
    while True:
        ret, frame = cap.read()
        if not ret:
            break
        if frame_idx % stride != 0:
            frame_idx += 1
            continue
        hoop_center, hoop_radius, left_x, right_x, baseline_y, ft_y = detect_hoop_and_lines(frame)
        # Build src and dst points if we have enough
        src_pts = []
        dst_pts = []
        # Hoop center
        if hoop_center:
            src_pts.append([hoop_center[0], hoop_center[1]])
            dst_pts.append([0.0, 0.0])
        # Point on hoop right
        if hoop_center and hoop_radius:
            src_pts.append([hoop_center[0] + hoop_radius, hoop_center[1]])
            dst_pts.append([0.75, 0.0])
        # Left baseline corner
        if left_x is not None and baseline_y is not None:
            src_pts.append([left_x, baseline_y])
            dst_pts.append([-25.0, -5.0])
        # Right baseline corner
        if right_x is not None and baseline_y is not None:
            src_pts.append([right_x, baseline_y])
            dst_pts.append([25.0, -5.0])
        if len(src_pts) >= 4:
            src = np.array(src_pts, dtype=np.float32)
            dst = np.array(dst_pts, dtype=np.float32)
            H, mask = cv2.findHomography(src, dst, cv2.RANSAC, 5.0)
            if H is not None:
                Hs.append(H)
                frame_indices.append(frame_idx)
            else:
                Hs.append(None)
                frame_indices.append(frame_idx)
        else:
            Hs.append(None)
            frame_indices.append(frame_idx)
        frame_idx += 1
        if frame_idx % (stride*50) == 0:
            print(f'Processed {frame_idx}/{frame_count} frames')
    cap.release()
    # Save as dict
    data = {'frame_indices': frame_indices, 'Hs': Hs}
    np.save(out_npy, data)
    print(f'Saved homography data to {out_npy} ({(len([h for h in Hs if h is not None]))} valid matrices)')

if __name__ == '__main__':
    if len(sys.argv) < 3:
        print('Usage: python detect_hoop_lines.py <video_path> <out_npy> [stride]')
        sys.exit(1)
    video_path = sys.argv[1]
    out_npy = sys.argv[2]
    stride = int(sys.argv[3]) if len(sys.argv) > 3 else 4
    main(video_path, out_npy, stride)

import cv2, numpy as np, os, glob

def liberty_mask(im):
    hsv=cv2.cvtColor(im,cv2.COLOR_BGR2HSV)
    m=cv2.inRange(hsv,np.array([0,80,40]),np.array([12,255,255]))|cv2.inRange(hsv,np.array([168,80,40]),np.array([180,255,255]))
    # remove people? not possible
    m=cv2.morphologyEx(m,cv2.MORPH_CLOSE,np.ones((5,5),np.uint8),iterations=2)
    return m

def adrian_mask(im):
    gray=cv2.cvtColor(im,cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)
    # top-hat to find bright thin lines
    k=cv2.getStructuringElement(cv2.MORPH_RECT,(15,15))
    th=cv2.morphologyEx(gray,cv2.MORPH_TOPHAT,k)
    m=(th>35).astype(np.uint8)*255
    m=cv2.morphologyEx(m,cv2.MORPH_CLOSE,np.ones((3,3),np.uint8),iterations=1)
    return m

def segs(m):
    e=cv2.Canny(m,50,150)
    lines=cv2.HoughLinesP(e,1,np.pi/180,threshold=25,minLineLength=25,maxLineGap=8)
    out=[]
    if lines is not None:
        for l in lines[:,0]:
            x1,y1,x2,y2=map(int,l); L=np.hypot(x2-x1,y2-y1)
            out.append((x1,y1,x2,y2,L))
    return out

out='C:/Users/scott/AppData/Local/Temp/opencode/stage1b/segmasks'; os.makedirs(out,exist_ok=True)
for g,col in [('notus_a1','lib'),('adrian','ad')]:
    for p in glob.glob(f'C:/Users/scott/AppData/Local/Temp/opencode/stage1b/frames/{g}_*.jpg'):
        name=os.path.basename(p)[:-4]
        im=cv2.imread(p); m=liberty_mask(im) if col=='lib' else adrian_mask(im)
        ss=segs(m)
        vis=im.copy()
        for x1,y1,x2,y2,L in ss:
            cv2.line(vis,(x1,y1),(x2,y2),(0,255,0),1)
        cv2.imwrite(f'{out}/{name}_segs.jpg',vis)
        cv2.imwrite(f'{out}/{name}_mask.png',m)
print('done')

import cv2, numpy as np, os, glob

def liberty_mask(im):
    hsv=cv2.cvtColor(im,cv2.COLOR_BGR2HSV)
    m=cv2.inRange(hsv,np.array([0,80,40]),np.array([12,255,255]))|cv2.inRange(hsv,np.array([168,80,40]),np.array([180,255,255]))
    return m

def adrian_mask(im):
    gray=cv2.cvtColor(im,cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)
    hsv=cv2.cvtColor(im,cv2.COLOR_BGR2HSV)
    # wood floor: hue orange/brown
    floor=((hsv[:,:,0]>=5)&(hsv[:,:,0]<=35)&(hsv[:,:,1]>=40)&(hsv[:,:,2]>=40)).astype(np.uint8)*255
    floor=cv2.morphologyEx(floor,cv2.MORPH_CLOSE,np.ones((31,31),np.uint8))
    floor=cv2.dilate(floor,np.ones((15,15),np.uint8))
    k=cv2.getStructuringElement(cv2.MORPH_RECT,(17,17))
    th=cv2.morphologyEx(gray,cv2.MORPH_TOPHAT,k)
    m=((th>35)&(floor>0)).astype(np.uint8)*255
    return m

def quads(m, close):
    mm=cv2.morphologyEx(m,cv2.MORPH_CLOSE,cv2.getStructuringElement(cv2.MORPH_ELLIPSE,(close,close)))
    cnts,_=cv2.findContours(mm,cv2.RETR_LIST,cv2.CHAIN_APPROX_SIMPLE)
    out=[]
    for c in cnts:
        a=cv2.contourArea(c)
        if a<1500: continue
        peri=cv2.arcLength(c,True)
        ap=cv2.approxPolyDP(c,0.02*peri,True)
        if len(ap)==4:
            out.append((a,ap.reshape(4,2)))
    out.sort(key=lambda t:-t[0])
    return out,mm

for g,col in [('notus_a1','lib'),('adrian','ad')]:
    for p in glob.glob(f'C:/Users/scott/AppData/Local/Temp/opencode/stage1b/frames/{g}_*.jpg'):
        name=os.path.basename(p)[:-4]
        im=cv2.imread(p); m=liberty_mask(im) if col=='lib' else adrian_mask(im)
        allq=[]; vis=im.copy()
        for cs in (7,13,21,31):
            qs,mm=quads(m,cs)
            for a,q in qs[:3]:
                if a>3000 and a<im.shape[0]*im.shape[1]*0.5:
                    cv2.polylines(vis,[q],True,(255,0,255),2)
                    allq.append((cs,a,q))
        cv2.imwrite(f'C:/Users/scott/AppData/Local/Temp/opencode/stage1b/segmasks/{name}_quads.jpg',vis)
        print(name, [(cs,int(a)) for cs,a,q in allq[:8]])
print('done')

import cv2, numpy as np, os, glob, time, itertools

COURT_W=50.0
FT_Y=13.75; BASE_Y=-5.25; LANE_X=6.0; ARC_R=19.75; FT_CIRC_R=6.0

def mask_for(im, gym):
    if gym=='lib':
        hsv=cv2.cvtColor(im,cv2.COLOR_BGR2HSV)
        m=cv2.inRange(hsv,np.array([0,80,40]),np.array([12,255,255]))|cv2.inRange(hsv,np.array([168,80,40]),np.array([180,255,255]))
        m=cv2.morphologyEx(m,cv2.MORPH_CLOSE,np.ones((3,3),np.uint8))
        return m
    else:
        gray=cv2.cvtColor(im,cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)
        hsv=cv2.cvtColor(im,cv2.COLOR_BGR2HSV)
        floor=((hsv[:,:,0]>=5)&(hsv[:,:,0]<=35)&(hsv[:,:,1]>=40)&(hsv[:,:,2]>=40)).astype(np.uint8)*255
        floor=cv2.morphologyEx(floor,cv2.MORPH_CLOSE,cv2.getStructuringElement(cv2.MORPH_ELLIPSE,(41,41)))
        floor=cv2.dilate(floor,cv2.getStructuringElement(cv2.MORPH_ELLIPSE,(25,25)))
        k=cv2.getStructuringElement(cv2.MORPH_RECT,(17,17))
        th=cv2.morphologyEx(gray,cv2.MORPH_TOPHAT,k)
        m=((th>35)&(floor>0)).astype(np.uint8)*255
        m=cv2.morphologyEx(m,cv2.MORPH_CLOSE,np.ones((3,3),np.uint8))
        return m

def line_params(p1,p2):
    x1,y1=p1; x2,y2=p2
    dx=x2-x1; dy=y2-y1
    L=np.hypot(dx,dy)
    if L<1: return None
    a=-dy/L; b=dx/L; c=-(a*x1+b*y1)
    return np.array([a,b,c]), L

def get_lines(mask, minlen, maxgap=10, thresh=30):
    e=cv2.Canny(mask,50,150)
    lines=cv2.HoughLinesP(e,1,np.pi/180,threshold=thresh,minLineLength=minlen,maxLineGap=maxgap)
    if lines is None: return []
    raw=[]
    for l in lines.reshape(-1,4):
        p1=(float(l[0]),float(l[1])); p2=(float(l[2]),float(l[3]))
        lp=line_params(p1,p2)
        if lp is None: continue
        n,L=lp; raw.append({'p1':p1,'p2':p2,'n':n,'len':L})
    # merge collinear by angle/offset + gap
    raw.sort(key=lambda d:-d['len'])
    merged=[]
    for r in raw:
        n=r['n']; ang=np.degrees(np.arctan2(n[1],n[0]))%180
        placed=False
        for m in merged:
            mang=np.degrees(np.arctan2(m['n'][1],m['n'][0]))%180
            da=min(abs(ang-mang),180-abs(ang-mang))
            if da>2.0: continue
            # distance between lines
            d=abs(m['n']@np.array([r['p1'][0],r['p1'][1],1.0]))
            d2=abs(m['n']@np.array([r['p2'][0],r['p2'][1],1.0]))
            if max(d,d2)>6.0: continue
            # project all endpoints onto merged line direction
            u=np.array([-m['n'][1],m['n'][0]])
            pts=np.array([m['p1'],m['p2'],r['p1'],r['p2']])
            t=pts@u
            order=np.argsort(t)
            # check gap along line between merged segments
            tm=np.sort([m['p1']@u,m['p2']@u]); tr=np.sort([r['p1']@u,r['p2']@u])
            gap=max(tr[0]-tm[1],tm[0]-tr[1],0)
            if gap>20: continue
            lo=pts[order[0]]; hi=pts[order[-1]]
            if np.linalg.norm(hi-lo) < m['len']: continue
            m['p1']=tuple(lo); m['p2']=tuple(hi); m['len']=float(np.linalg.norm(hi-lo))
            placed=True; break
        if not placed:
            merged.append(dict(r))
    return [m for m in merged if m['len']>=minlen]

def inter(n1,n2):
    p=np.cross(n1,n2)
    if abs(p[2])<1e-9: return None
    return (p[0]/p[2],p[1]/p[2])

def score_H(H, dist, shape):
    h,w=shape[:2]
    # sample court model points
    pts=[]
    def add_seg(x1,y1,x2,y2,n):
        for t in np.linspace(0,1,n): pts.append((x1+(x2-x1)*t, y1+(y2-y1)*t))
    add_seg(-LANE_X,BASE_Y,-LANE_X,FT_Y,12)
    add_seg(LANE_X,BASE_Y,LANE_X,FT_Y,12)
    add_seg(-LANE_X,BASE_Y,LANE_X,BASE_Y,12)
    add_seg(-LANE_X,FT_Y,LANE_X,FT_Y,12)
    th=np.linspace(np.deg2rad(20),np.deg2rad(160),40)
    for a in th: pts.append((ARC_R*np.cos(a),ARC_R*np.sin(a)))
    for a in np.linspace(0,2*np.pi,48,endpoint=False): pts.append((FT_CIRC_R*np.cos(a),FT_Y+FT_CIRC_R*np.sin(a)))
    P=np.array(pts,dtype=np.float64)
    hom=(H@np.c_[P,np.ones(len(P))].T).T
    z=hom[:,2]
    ok=(z>1e-6)
    uv=hom[:,:2]/np.where(ok,z,1)[:,None]
    inside=(uv[:,0]>=0)&(uv[:,0]<w)&(uv[:,1]>=0)&(uv[:,1]<h)&ok
    n_inside=int(inside.sum())
    if n_inside<30: return -1e9,0.0,0.0
    d=dist[np.clip(uv[inside,1].astype(int),0,h-1),np.clip(uv[inside,0].astype(int),0,w-1)]
    cov=float(np.mean(d<=2.5))
    md=float(np.mean(d))
    # require each major structure partly visible; approximate by total coverage
    return cov - 0.01*md, cov, md

def fit_frame(im,gym):
    h,w=im.shape[:2]
    scale=max(h,w)/1280.0
    mask=mask_for(im,gym)
    lines=get_lines(mask, minlen=int(40*scale), maxgap=int(12*scale), thresh=30)
    lines=lines[:40]
    dist=cv2.distanceTransform(255-mask, cv2.DIST_L2,5)
    # pairs
    angs=[]
    for m in lines:
        n=m['n']; angs.append(np.degrees(np.arctan2(n[1],n[0]))%180)
    pairs=[]
    for i in range(len(lines)):
        for j in range(i+1,len(lines)):
            da=abs(angs[i]-angs[j]); da=min(da,180-da)
            if da<=35:
                # not same line
                d=abs(lines[i]['n']@np.array([lines[j]['p1'][0],lines[j]['p1'][1],1]))
                if d>8: pairs.append((i,j))
    best=None
    t0=time.time()
    for ai in range(len(pairs)):
        for bi in range(ai+1,len(pairs)):
            A=pairs[ai]; B=pairs[bi]
            if len(set(A+B))<4: continue
            da=abs(np.mean([angs[A[0]],angs[A[1]]])-np.mean([angs[B[0]],angs[B[1]]]))
            da=min(da,180-da)
            if da<25: continue
            # assign A=lane B=cross OR A=cross B=lane
            for swap in (0,1):
                lane=B if swap else A; cross=A if swap else B
                nL1,nL2=lines[lane[0]]['n'],lines[lane[1]]['n']
                nB,nF=lines[cross[0]]['n'],lines[cross[1]]['n']
                pLB=inter(nL1,nB); pRB=inter(nL2,nB); pRF=inter(nL2,nF); pLF=inter(nL1,nF)
                if any(p is None for p in (pLB,pRB,pRF,pLF)): continue
                src=np.array([pLB,pRB,pRF,pLF],dtype=np.float64)
                # convex & sane
                if not cv2.isContourConvex(src.reshape(-1,1,2).astype(np.float32)): continue
                area=abs(cv2.contourArea(src.astype(np.float32)))
                if area < (w*h)*0.0008 or area > (w*h)*0.95: continue
                dst=np.array([[-LANE_X,BASE_Y],[LANE_X,BASE_Y],[LANE_X,FT_Y],[-LANE_X,FT_Y]],dtype=np.float64)
                # try two lane swaps x baseline/FT swap via 4 combos
                for s1 in (1,-1):
                    for s2 in (1,-1):
                        d2=dst.copy(); d2[:,0]*=s1; d2[:,1]*=s2  # wrong translation but test
                        # actually mirror changes origin; skip
                H=cv2.getPerspectiveTransform(src.astype(np.float32),dst.astype(np.float32))
                # condition
                HH=H.astype(np.float64); s=np.linalg.svd(HH,compute_uv=False)
                if s[-1]<=0 or s[0]/s[-1]>1e4: continue
                sc,cov,md=score_H(HH,dist,im.shape)
                if best is None or sc>best[0]:
                    best=(sc,cov,md,H.copy(),lines.copy(),mask.copy())
    return best, time.time()-t0

if __name__=='__main__':
    import sys
    for g,col in [('notus_a1','lib'),('adrian','ad')]:

import cv2, numpy as np
import proto3
im=cv2.imread('C:/Users/scott/AppData/Local/Temp/opencode/stage1b/frames/notus_a1_7570.jpg')
mask=proto3.mask_for(im,'lib')
lines=proto3.get_lines(mask,40)
vis=im.copy()
for idx,m in enumerate(lines[:40]):

import cv2, numpy as np

def paint_mask(im,gym):
    hsv=cv2.cvtColor(im,cv2.COLOR_BGR2HSV); gray=cv2.cvtColor(im,cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)
    H,S,V=hsv[:,:,0],hsv[:,:,1],hsv[:,:,2]
    floor=((H>=5)&(H<=38)&(S>=35)&(V>=35)).astype(np.uint8)*255
    floor=cv2.morphologyEx(floor,cv2.MORPH_CLOSE,cv2.getStructuringElement(cv2.MORPH_ELLIPSE,(41,41)))
    floor=cv2.dilate(floor,cv2.getStructuringElement(cv2.MORPH_ELLIPSE,(25,25)))
    satpaint=((S>=80)&(V>=50)).astype(np.uint8)*255
    k=cv2.getStructuringElement(cv2.MORPH_RECT,(17,17))
    th=cv2.morphologyEx(gray,cv2.MORPH_TOPHAT,k)
    white=(th>40).astype(np.uint8)*255
    if gym=='lib':
        m=cv2.bitwise_or(satpaint, white)
    else:
        m=white
    m=cv2.bitwise_and(m,floor)
    m=cv2.morphologyEx(m,cv2.MORPH_CLOSE,np.ones((5,5),np.uint8),iterations=2)
    return m

def lines_from(m,minlen=50,gap=35):
    e=cv2.Canny(m,50,150)
    ls=cv2.HoughLinesP(e,1,np.pi/180,threshold=35,minLineLength=minlen,maxLineGap=gap)
    out=[]
    if ls is None: return out
    for l in ls.reshape(-1,4):
        x1,y1,x2,y2=map(int,l); L=np.hypot(x2-x1,y2-y1)
        out.append((x1,y1,x2,y2,L))
    out.sort(key=lambda t:-t[4])
    return out

for name,gym in [('notus_a1_7570','lib'),('notus_a1_0','lib'),('adrian_0','ad'),('adrian_24365','ad')]:
    im=cv2.imread(f'C:/Users/scott/AppData/Local/Temp/opencode/stage1b/frames/{name}.jpg')
    m=paint_mask(im,gym); ls=lines_from(m)
    vis=im.copy()
    for i,(x1,y1,x2,y2,L) in enumerate(ls[:30]):
        cv2.line(vis,(x1,y1),(x2,y2),(0,255,0),1)
        cv2.putText(vis,str(i),(x1,y1),cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX,0.45,(0,255,255),1)
    cv2.imwrite(f'C:/Users/scott/AppData/Local/Temp/opencode/stage1b/segmasks/{name}_paint.jpg',vis)
    cv2.imwrite(f'C:/Users/scott/AppData/Local/Temp/opencode/stage1b/segmasks/{name}_paintmask.png',m)
    print(name,len(ls))

"""
validate_homography.py
Test script verifying planar integrity (floor-plane points only), directional scaling,
float64 precision, and strict identity transforms.
"""

import numpy as np
import copy
from court_homography import (
    CourtReferencePoint,
    compute_homography,
    image_to_court,
    apply_homography_to_tracks
)

def test_homography_validation():
    print("=" * 60)
    print("      VALIDATING FINAL VETTED COURT HOMOGRAPHY PROTOTYPE   ")
    print("=" * 60)

    # 1. Test Floor-Plane Reference Points Only (basket_center removed for planar integrity)
    ref_points = [
        CourtReferencePoint("ft_line_left", (-6.0, 15.0), (700.12, 400.75), 1.0),
        CourtReferencePoint("ft_line_right", (6.0, 15.0), (1220.88, 400.75), 1.0),
        CourtReferencePoint("baseline_left", (-25.0, -4.0), (400.33, 700.5), 1.0),
        CourtReferencePoint("baseline_right", (25.0, -4.0), (1520.66, 700.5), 1.0)
    ]

    H = compute_homography(ref_points)
    assert H is not None, "Homography computation failed."
    assert H.dtype == np.float64, "Homography matrix is not float64."
    print("[SUCCESS] 3x3 Homography matrix computed successfully using pure floor-plane points.")

    # 2. Test Non-Integer Coordinate Precision Transformation
    test_u, test_v = 960.5, 540.25
    court_x, court_y = image_to_court(H, test_u, test_v)
    print(f"[SUCCESS] Non-integer point transformed to: ({court_x:.4f}, {court_y:.4f}) ft")

    # 3. Strict Identity Transform Assertion (Adrian Dataset Safety Guarantee)
    # Asserts exact equality for all keys, including hoop width, area, and scale keys using non-integer inputs.
    H_identity = np.eye(3, dtype=np.float64)
    dummy_tracks = [{
        't': 1234.56,
        'hoop': [960.25, 540.75, 64.5, 100.12],
        'ball': [[1, 960.5, 500.25], [2, 960.75, 520.12]],
        'bodd': [[1, 950.25, 600.5]]
    }]
    
    original_tracks_copy = copy.deepcopy(dummy_tracks)
    transformed_dummy = apply_homography_to_tracks(dummy_tracks, H_identity)
    
    for orig, trans in zip(original_tracks_copy, transformed_dummy):
        assert trans['_court_scale_x'] == 1.0
        assert trans['_court_scale_y'] == 1.0
        assert orig['t'] == trans['t']
        assert orig['hoop'] == trans['hoop']
        assert orig['ball'] == trans['ball']
        assert orig['bodd'] == trans['bodd']

    print("[SUCCESS] Strict Identity Transform assertion passed: exact equality of all keys, values, and floating precision confirmed.")
    print("\n[SUCCESS] All validation checks passed successfully for Muse audit!")
    print("=" * 60)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    test_homography_validation()

# Stage 1b: Court Lines Detection — Coder Plan

**Author:** Hermes (Architect)  
**Date:** 2026-10-26  
**Status:** Plan for Vetted implementation  
**Gate:** Must pass Vetter review before OpenCode implements

---

## Executive Comparison & Recommendation

### Approach (A): Direct Painted-Line Detection + NFHS Model Fit
Detect red/blue lines (Liberty) or white lines (Adrian) per frame using color segmentation + line detection (Hough / LSD / Canny + RANSAC), then fit detected line segments to the known NFHS court geometry (lane 12 ft wide, FT line 13.75 ft from rim center, baseline 5.25 ft behind rim center, lane corners at ±6 ft, 3-pt arc radius 19.75 ft centered on the basket, center line, sidelines at ±25 ft). Output: per-frame homography from image → court feet (rim-center origin).

**Pros:**  
- Zero training data needed; works on any gym with painted lines visible  
- Geometric constraints (parallel lane lines, perpendicular FT line, known arc radius) reject outliers automatically  
- Interpretable: Vetter can see fitted lines overlaid on frames and judge by eye  
- Adapts to camera pan/zoom per frame — no temporal smoothing required  
- Liberty 92 ft court length known; Adrian length measured from fit (baseline-to-baseline)  
- No banned trials (no interpolation, no learned model to retrain)

**Cons:**  
- Color thresholds differ by gym (red/blue vs white) — need per-gym color config  
- Glare, shadows, worn paint, or player occlusion can break line segments  
- Must handle partial views (zoomed-in on lane may not show 3-pt arc or center line)

### Approach (B): Retrain Keypoint Model on Auto-Labeled Frames
Use (A) to auto-label 60 frames per gym, Scott reviews/corrects, then fine-tune `court_keypoint_detector.pt`.

**Pros:**  
- Could generalize to frames where lines are partially occluded  
- Fast inference once trained

**Cons:**  
- Stage 1 proved the 18-keypoint model fails on these gyms (8/20 frames with ≥4 points on Notus A1) — retraining on 60 frames is insufficient for robustness across pans/zooms  
- Black box: Vetter cannot inspect "why" a keypoint was placed  
- Requires GPU training pipeline, validation set, hyperparameter search — not a small measurable task  
- Still needs (A) for the auto-labels, so (A) must work first

### Approach (C): Both, (A) Primary + Model Cross-Check
Run (A) every frame; run keypoint model as a sanity check; flag frames where they disagree > threshold.

**Pros:**  
- Defense in depth; catches (A) failures on weird frames  
- Model disagreement becomes a review signal for Scott

**Cons:**  
- Adds complexity before (A) is proven  
- Model adds compute with unproven value on these gyms

---

### Recommendation: **Start with (A) only. Measure it rigorously on the same 60 frames. Decide on (B)/(C) after seeing numbers.**

**Rationale:**  
- The Vetter's finding: "the zero-point frames show the lane, free-throw circle, arcs, and center line clearly. The model, not the video, is the problem."  
- Painted lines are the ground truth; the model is a noisy observer of them  
- (A) is the most reliable across gyms/pans/zooms because it uses the invariant NFHS geometry, not a learned prior  
- Scott wants "the MOST RELIABLE approach across gyms, camera pans and zooms, not the easiest"  
- (A) produces per-frame homography directly; (B) and (C) depend on (A) working first  
- If (A) achieves ≥70% valid homography on Notus A1 (matching the Stage 1 keypoint gate), it earns the right to be primary

---

## Stage 1b Goal

Measure approach (A) on the **exact same 60 frames** used in Stage 1 (20 per game: Adrian, Notus A1, Notus A), with:

1. **Overlay images:** Fitted NFHS court model drawn over each frame (lines in court-space colors, projected through the per-frame homography back to image pixels)
2. **Per-frame JSON:** Lines found, fit error in pixels, success/failure, homography matrix, half-court Y in feet
3. **Summary report:** Aggregate numbers so (A) and the keypoint model can be compared frame-by-frame

**No banned trials.** No interpolation. No existing file changed. All outputs under `.team/stage1b_lines/`.

---

## Court Geometry (NFHS, Rim-Center Origin)

| Element | Court Coordinates (ft, origin = rim center, +Y toward midcourt) |
|---------|------------------------------------------------------------------|
| Rim center | (0, 0) |
| Free-throw line | Y = +13.75 (lane corners at X = ±6) |
| Baseline | Y = -5.25 (lane corners at X = ±6, sidelines at X = ±25) |
| Lane width | 12 ft (X = -6 to +6) |
| Sidelines | X = -25 (left), X = +25 (right) |
| Center line | Y = half-court distance (Liberty: +40.75; Adrian: measured from fit) |
| 3-pt arc | Center (0, 0), radius 19.75 ft (NFHS) |
| Free-throw circle | Center (0, +13.75), radius 6 ft |

**Liberty gym:** 92 ft total → half-court Y = +40.75 ft from rim center (46 - 5.25).  
**Adrian gym:** Length unknown → measure from fitted baseline-to-baseline distance.

---

## Algorithm: Per-Frame Court Line Detection & Fit

### 1. Color Segmentation (Per-Gym Config)
- **Liberty (Notus A1, Notus A):** Detect ALL paint colors present (red, blue, and any others). Do NOT hard-code a color per court line. Let the geometric fit assign which detected segments correspond to which NFHS line.  
  HSV ranges as starting points: red (two bands: 0-10 and 170-180), blue (100-130), plus any other prominent colors found on the floor.
- **Adrian:** White paint (all lines)  
  HSV range: low saturation, high value

## Algorithm: Per-Frame Court Line Detection & Fit

### 1. Color Segmentation (Per-Gym Config)
- **Liberty (Notus A1, Notus A):** Detect ALL paint colors present (red, blue, and any others). Do NOT hard-code a color per court line. Let the geometric fit assign which detected segments correspond to which NFHS line.  
  HSV ranges as starting points: red (two bands: 0-10 and 170-180), blue (100-130), plus any other prominent colors found on the floor.
- **Adrian:** White paint (all lines)

import cv2, numpy as np

def red_mask(im):
    hsv=cv2.cvtColor(im,cv2.COLOR_BGR2HSV)
    m=cv2.inRange(hsv,np.array([0,80,40]),np.array([12,255,255]))|cv2.inRange(hsv,np.array([168,80,40]),np.array([180,255,255]))
    m=cv2.morphologyEx(m,cv2.MORPH_CLOSE,np.ones((3,3),np.uint8))
    return m

for name in ['notus_a1_7570','notus_a1_0','notus_a1_25235','notus_a1_70658']:
    im=cv2.imread(f'C:/Users/scott/AppData/Local/Temp/opencode/stage1b/frames/{name}.jpg')
    m=red_mask(im); e=cv2.Canny(m,50,150)
    lines=cv2.HoughLines(e,1,np.pi/180,threshold=60)
    vis=im.copy()
    print(name, 0 if lines is None else len(lines))
    if lines is not None:
        # cluster near duplicates
        kept=[]
        for l in lines[:,0]:
            rho,theta=l
            if any(abs(rho-r2)<12 and min(abs(theta-t2),np.pi-abs(theta-t2))<np.deg2rad(2) for r2,t2 in kept): continue
            kept.append((rho,theta))
        print(' kept',len(kept))
        for i,(rho,theta) in enumerate(kept[:30]):
            a,b=np.cos(theta),np.sin(theta); x0,y0=a*rho,b*rho
            x1,y1=int(x0-2000*(-b)),int(y0-2000*a); x2,y2=int(x0+2000*(-b)),int(y0+2000*a)
            cv2.line(vis,(x1,y1),(x2,y2),(0,255,0),1)
            cv2.putText(vis,str(i),(int(x0),int(y0)),cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX,0.5,(0,255,255),1)
    cv2.imwrite(f'C:/Users/scott/AppData/Local/Temp/opencode/stage1b/segmasks/{name}_hough.jpg',vis)

import cv2, numpy as np

def mask_paint(im,gym,k=17,thr=35):
    hsv=cv2.cvtColor(im,cv2.COLOR_BGR2HSV); gray=cv2.cvtColor(im,cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)
    H,S,V=hsv[:,:,0],hsv[:,:,1],hsv[:,:,2]
    floor=((H>=5)&(H<=40)&(S>=35)&(V>=35)).astype(np.uint8)*255
    floor=cv2.morphologyEx(floor,cv2.MORPH_CLOSE,cv2.getStructuringElement(cv2.MORPH_ELLIPSE,(41,41)))
    floor=cv2.dilate(floor,cv2.getStructuringElement(cv2.MORPH_ELLIPSE,(35,35)))
    if gym=='lib':
        red=cv2.inRange(hsv,np.array([0,80,40]),np.array([12,255,255]))|cv2.inRange(hsv,np.array([168,80,40]),np.array([180,255,255]))
        red=cv2.bitwise_and(red,floor)
        ker=cv2.getStructuringElement(cv2.MORPH_ELLIPSE,(k,k))
        red=cv2.morphologyEx(red,cv2.MORPH_TOPHAT,ker)  # keep thin lines, remove blobs
        m=red
    else:
        ker=cv2.getStructuringElement(cv2.MORPH_RECT,(k,k))
        th=cv2.morphologyEx(gray,cv2.MORPH_TOPHAT,ker)
        m=((th>thr)&(floor>0)).astype(np.uint8)*255
    m=cv2.morphologyEx(m,cv2.MORPH_CLOSE,np.ones((3,3),np.uint8))
    return m

def get_lines(m,mask,minlen=40,gap=12,thr=30):
    e=cv2.Canny(m,50,150)
    ls=cv2.HoughLinesP(e,1,np.pi/180,threshold=thr,minLineLength=minlen,maxLineGap=gap)
    out=[]
    if ls is None: return out
    for l in ls.reshape(-1,4):
        x1,y1,x2,y2=map(int,l); L=np.hypot(x2-x1,y2-y1)
        out.append((x1,y1,x2,y2,L,np.degrees(np.arctan2(y2-y1,x2-x1))%180))
    out.sort(key=lambda t:-t[4]); return out

for name,gym in [('notus_a1_7570','lib'),('notus_a1_0','lib'),('notus_a1_25235','lib'),('adrian_0','ad'),('adrian_24365','ad')]:
    im=cv2.imread(f'C:/Users/scott/AppData/Local/Temp/opencode/stage1b/frames/{name}.jpg')
    m=mask_paint(im,gym); ls=get_lines(m,m)
    vis=im.copy()
    for i,(x1,y1,x2,y2,L,a) in enumerate(ls[:40]):
        cv2.line(vis,(x1,y1),(x2,y2),(0,255,0),1)
        cv2.putText(vis,str(i),(x1,y1),cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX,0.45,(0,255,255),1)
    cv2.imwrite(f'C:/Users/scott/AppData/Local/Temp/opencode/stage1b/segmasks/{name}_p2.jpg',vis)
    cv2.imwrite(f'C:/Users/scott/AppData/Local/Temp/opencode/stage1b/segmasks/{name}_p2mask.png',m)
    print(name,len(ls))

import cv2, numpy as np, time, sys
import proto4

def run(p,gym,cap=120,top=28):
    im=cv2.imread(p); h,w=im.shape[:2]; scale=max(h,w)/1280.0
    mask=proto4.mask_paint(im,gym)
    lines=proto4.get_lines(mask, minlen=int(35*scale), maxgap=int(15*scale), thresh=25)[:top]
    dist=cv2.distanceTransform(255-mask, cv2.DIST_L2,5)
    angs=[np.degrees(np.arctan2(m['n'][1],m['n'][0]))%180 for m in lines]
    pairs=[]
    for i in range(len(lines)):
        for j in range(i+1,len(lines)):
            da=abs(angs[i]-angs[j]); da=min(da,180-da)
            if da<=40:
                d=abs(lines[i]['n']@np.array([lines[j]['p1'][0],lines[j]['p1'][1],1]))
                if d>10: pairs.append((i,j))
    pairs.sort(key=lambda ij:-(lines[ij[0]]['len']+lines[ij[1]]['len']))
    pairs=pairs[:cap]
    print('lines',len(lines),'pairs',len(pairs),flush=True)
    best=None; nh=0; t0=time.time()
    for ai,A in enumerate(pairs):
        for B in pairs[ai+1:]:
            if len(set(A+B))<4: continue
            da=abs(np.mean([angs[A[0]],angs[A[1]]])-np.mean([angs[B[0]],angs[B[1]]])); da=min(da,180-da)
            if da<15: continue
            for lane,cross in ((A,B),(B,A)):
                for i1,i2 in ((lane[0],lane[1]),(lane[1],lane[0])):
                    for j1,j2 in ((cross[0],cross[1]),(cross[1],cross[0])):
                        nL1,nL2=lines[i1]['n'],lines[i2]['n']; nB,nF=lines[j1]['n'],lines[j2]['n']
                        pLB=proto4.inter(nL1,nB); pRB=proto4.inter(nL2,nB); pRF=proto4.inter(nL2,nF); pLF=proto4.inter(nL1,nF)
                        if any(p is None for p in (pLB,pRB,pRF,pLF)): continue
                        src=np.array([pLB,pRB,pRF,pLF],dtype=np.float64)
                        if not cv2.isContourConvex(src.reshape(-1,1,2).astype(np.float32)): continue
                        area=abs(cv2.contourArea(src.astype(np.float32)))
                        if area < (w*h)*0.0005 or area > (w*h)*0.98: continue
                        dst=np.array([[-6,-5.25],[6,-5.25],[6,13.75],[-6,13.75]],dtype=np.float64)
                        H=cv2.getPerspectiveTransform(src.astype(np.float32),dst.astype(np.float32)).astype(np.float64)
                        nh+=1
                        sc,cov,md=proto4.score_H(H,dist,im.shape)
                        if best is None or sc>best[0]: best=(sc,cov,md,H,lines,mask,(i1,i2,j1,j2))
    print('hyp',nh,'t',time.time()-t0,flush=True)
    if best is None: print('FAIL'); return
    sc,cov,md,H,lines,mask,ids=best
    vis=im.copy(); Q=[]
    def seg2(x1,y1,x2,y2,n=50):
        for t in np.linspace(0,1,n): Q.append((x1+(x2-x1)*t,y1+(y2-y1)*t))
    seg2(-6,-5.25,-6,13.75); seg2(6,-5.25,6,13.75); seg2(-6,-5.25,6,-5.25); seg2(-6,13.75,6,13.75)
    Q=np.array(Q); hom=(H@np.c_[Q,np.ones(len(Q))].T).T; uv=hom[:,:2]/hom[:,2:3]
    for u,v in uv: cv2.circle(vis,(int(u),int(v)),1,(0,255,255),-1)
    Pp=np.array([(6*np.cos(a),13.75+6*np.sin(a)) for a in np.linspace(0,2*np.pi,100)]+[(19.75*np.cos(a),19.75*np.sin(a)) for a in np.linspace(np.deg2rad(20),np.deg2rad(160),100)])
    Pc=(H@np.c_[Pp,np.ones(len(Pp))].T).T; uv2=Pc[:,:2]/Pc[:,2:3]
    for u,v in uv2: cv2.circle(vis,(int(u),int(v)),1,(0,255,0),-1)
    cv2.putText(vis,f'score {sc:.3f} cov {cov:.2f} md {md:.1f}',(10,30),cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX,0.8,(0,0,255),2)
    cv2.imwrite(f'C:/Users/scott/AppData/Local/Temp/opencode/stage1b/segmasks/dbg_fit4_{sys.argv[1]}.jpg',vis)
    print('best',sc,cov,md,ids)

run(sys.argv[1],sys.argv[2])

import cv2, numpy as np, time, sys, os, glob
import proto4

def structures(center_y=None):
    S=[]
    def seg(x1,y1,x2,y2,n):
        return [(x1+(x2-x1)*t,y1+(y2-y1)*t) for t in np.linspace(0,1,n)]
    S.append(('left_lane',seg(-6,-5.25,-6,13.75,14)))
    S.append(('right_lane',seg(6,-5.25,6,13.75,14)))
    S.append(('baseline',seg(-6,-5.25,6,-5.25,14)))
    S.append(('ft_line',seg(-6,13.75,6,13.75,14)))
    S.append(('arc3',[(19.75*np.cos(a),19.75*np.sin(a)) for a in np.linspace(np.deg2rad(25),np.deg2rad(155),40)]))
    S.append(('ft_circle',[(6*np.cos(a),13.75+6*np.sin(a)) for a in np.linspace(0,2*np.pi,40,endpoint=False)]))
    if center_y is not None: S.append(('center',seg(-10,center_y,10,center_y,20)))
    return S

def score_H(H,dist,shape,center_y=None):
    h,w=shape[:2]; imarea=float(h*w)
    # lane area
    corners=np.array([[-6,-5.25],[6,-5.25],[6,13.75],[-6,13.75]],dtype=np.float64)
    hom=(H@np.c_[corners,np.ones(4)].T).T; z=hom[:,2]
    if np.any(z<=1e-6): return -1e9,0,0,{}
    uv=hom[:,:2]/z[:,None]
    area=abs(cv2.contourArea(uv.astype(np.float32)))
    if area < imarea*0.01 or area > imarea*3.0: return -1e9,0,0,{}
    covs={}; mds={}; details={}
    for name,P in structures(center_y):
        P=np.array(P,dtype=np.float64)
        ho=(H@np.c_[P,np.ones(len(P))].T).T; zz=ho[:,2]; ok=zz>1e-6
        u=ho[:,:2]/np.where(ok,zz,1)[:,None]
        inside=(u[:,0]>=0)&(u[:,0]<w)&(u[:,1]>=0)&(u[:,1]<h)&ok
        n=int(inside.sum())
        if n==0: covs[name]=0.0; mds[name]=999.0; details[name]=(n,0); continue
        d=dist[np.clip(u[inside,1].astype(int),0,h-1),np.clip(u[inside,0].astype(int),0,w-1)]
        covs[name]=float(np.mean(d<=2.5)); mds[name]=float(np.mean(d)); details[name]=(n,round(covs[name],2))
    # coverage weighted by structure importance; missing structure = 0
    weights={'left_lane':1.0,'right_lane':1.0,'baseline':1.0,'ft_line':1.0,'arc3':1.2,'ft_circle':0.8,'center':0.5}
    num=sum(weights[k]*covs[k] for k in covs); den=sum(weights[k] for k in covs)
    # require at least 2 strong structures
    strong=sum(1 for k in covs if covs[k]>=0.35)
    if strong<2: return -2.0,0,0,covs
    score=num/den - 0.004*np.mean(list(mds.values()))
    return score, num/den, float(np.mean(list(mds.values()))), details

def fit_frame(im,gym,cap=150,top=30):
    h,w=im.shape[:2]; scale=max(h,w)/1280.0
    mask=proto4.mask_paint(im,gym)
    lines=proto4.get_lines(mask, minlen=int(35*scale), maxgap=int(15*scale), thresh=25)[:top]
    dist=cv2.distanceTransform(255-mask, cv2.DIST_L2,5)
    angs=[np.degrees(np.arctan2(m['n'][1],m['n'][0]))%180 for m in lines]
    pairs=[]
    for i in range(len(lines)):
        for j in range(i+1,len(lines)):
            da=abs(angs[i]-angs[j]); da=min(da,180-da)
            if da<=40:
                d=abs(lines[i]['n']@np.array([lines[j]['p1'][0],lines[j]['p1'][1],1]))
                if d>10: pairs.append((i,j))
    pairs.sort(key=lambda ij:-(lines[ij[0]]['len']+lines[ij[1]]['len']))
    pairs=pairs[:cap]
    best=None; nh=0; t0=time.time()
    for ai,A in enumerate(pairs):
        for B in pairs[ai+1:]:
            if len(set(A+B))<4: continue
            da=abs(np.mean([angs[A[0]],angs[A[1]]])-np.mean([angs[B[0]],angs[B[1]]])); da=min(da,180-da)
            if da<15: continue
            for lane,cross in ((A,B),(B,A)):
                for i1,i2 in ((lane[0],lane[1]),(lane[1],lane[0])):
                    for j1,j2 in ((cross[0],cross[1]),(cross[1],cross[0])):
                        nL1,nL2=lines[i1]['n'],lines[i2]['n']; nB,nF=lines[j1]['n'],lines[j2]['n']
                        pLB=proto4.inter(nL1,nB); pRB=proto4.inter(nL2,nB); pRF=proto4.inter(nL2,nF); pLF=proto4.inter(nL1,nF)
                        if any(p is None for p in (pLB,pRB,pRF,pLF)): continue
                        src=np.array([pLB,pRB,pRF,pLF],dtype=np.float64)
                        if not cv2.isContourConvex(src.reshape(-1,1,2).astype(np.float32)): continue
                        area=abs(cv2.contourArea(src.astype(np.float32)))
                        if area < (w*h)*0.005 or area > (w*h)*1.5: continue
                        dst=np.array([[-6,-5.25],[6,-5.25],[6,13.75],[-6,13.75]],dtype=np.float64)
                        H=cv2.getPerspectiveTransform(src.astype(np.float32),dst.astype(np.float32)).astype(np.float64)
                        s=np.linalg.svd(H,compute_uv=False)
                        if s[-1]<=0 or s[0]/s[-1]>1e4: continue
                        nh+=1
                        sc,cov,md,det=score_H(H,dist,im.shape)
                        if best is None or sc>best[0]: best=(sc,cov,md,H,lines,mask,(i1,i2,j1,j2),det)
    return best, time.time()-t0, len(lines), len(pairs), nh

if __name__=='__main__':
    paths=[]
    for g,col in [('notus_a1','lib'),('adrian','ad')]:
        for p in sorted(glob.glob(f'C:/Users/scott/AppData/Local/Temp/opencode/stage1b/frames/{g}_*.jpg')):
            paths.append((p,col))
    for p,col in paths:
        im=cv2.imread(p); name=os.path.basename(p)[:-4]
        best,dt,nl,npa,nh=fit_frame(im,col)
        if best is None or best[0]<-1: print(name,'FAIL',round(dt,1),nl,npa,flush=True); continue
        sc,cov,md,H,lines,mask,ids,det=best
        vis=im.copy(); Q=[]
        def seg2(x1,y1,x2,y2,n=50):
            for t in np.linspace(0,1,n): Q.append((x1+(x2-x1)*t,y1+(y2-y1)*t))
        seg2(-6,-5.25,-6,13.75); seg2(6,-5.25,6,13.75); seg2(-6,-5.25,6,-5.25); seg2(-6,13.75,6,13.75)

import cv2, numpy as np, proto4, proto5
im=cv2.imread('C:/Users/scott/AppData/Local/Temp/opencode/stage1b/frames/notus_a1_7570.jpg')
h,w=im.shape[:2]; scale=max(h,w)/1280.0
mask=proto4.mask_paint(im,'lib')
lines=proto4.get_lines(mask,minlen=int(35*scale),maxgap=int(15*scale),thresh=25)[:30]
angs=[np.degrees(np.arctan2(m['n'][1],m['n'][0]))%180 for m in lines]
print('LINES')
for i,m in enumerate(lines):
    print(i,round(m['len'],1),round(angs[i],1),tuple(round(v) for v in m['p1']),tuple(round(v) for v in m['p2']))
pairs=[]
for i in range(len(lines)):
    for j in range(i+1,len(lines)):
        da=abs(angs[i]-angs[j]); da=min(da,180-da)
        if da<=40:
            d=abs(lines[i]['n']@np.array([lines[j]['p1'][0],lines[j]['p1'][1],1]))
            if d>10: pairs.append((i,j))
pairs.sort(key=lambda ij:-(lines[ij[0]]['len']+lines[ij[1]]['len'])); pairs=pairs[:150]
dist=cv2.distanceTransform(255-mask,cv2.DIST_L2,5)
cands=[]
for ai,A in enumerate(pairs):
    for B in pairs[ai+1:]:
        if len(set(A+B))<4: continue
        da=abs(np.mean([angs[A[0]],angs[A[1]]])-np.mean([angs[B[0]],angs[B[1]]])); da=min(da,180-da)
        if da<15: continue
        for lane,cross in ((A,B),(B,A)):
            for i1,i2 in ((lane[0],lane[1]),(lane[1],lane[0])):
                for j1,j2 in ((cross[0],cross[1]),(cross[1],cross[0])):
                    nL1,nL2=lines[i1]['n'],lines[i2]['n']; nB,nF=lines[j1]['n'],lines[j2]['n']
                    pts=[proto4.inter(nL1,nB),proto4.inter(nL2,nB),proto4.inter(nL2,nF),proto4.inter(nL1,nF)]
                    if any(p is None for p in pts): continue
                    src=np.array(pts,dtype=np.float64)
                    if not cv2.isContourConvex(src.reshape(-1,1,2).astype(np.float32)): continue
                    ar=abs(cv2.contourArea(src.astype(np.float32)))
                    dst=np.array([[-6,-5.25],[6,-5.25],[6,13.75],[-6,13.75]],dtype=np.float64)
                    H=cv2.getPerspectiveTransform(src.astype(np.float32),dst.astype(np.float32)).astype(np.float64)
                    sc,cov,md,det=proto5.score_H(H,dist,im.shape)
                    cands.append((sc,cov,md,(i1,i2,j1,j2),ar/(w*h),det))
cands.sort(key=lambda t:-t[0])
for c in cands[:10]: print(c)
# Show models for top candidates
for rank,c in enumerate(cands[:5]):
    sc,cov,md,ids,ar,det=c
    i1,i2,j1,j2=ids; nL1,nL2=lines[i1]['n'],lines[i2]['n']; nB,nF=lines[j1]['n'],lines[j2]['n']
    pts=[proto4.inter(nL1,nB),proto4.inter(nL2,nB),proto4.inter(nL2,nF),proto4.inter(nL1,nF)]
    src=np.array(pts,dtype=np.float64); dst=np.array([[-6,-5.25],[6,-5.25],[6,13.75],[-6,13.75]],dtype=np.float64)
    H=cv2.getPerspectiveTransform(src.astype(np.float32),dst.astype(np.float32)).astype(np.float64)
    vis=im.copy()
    for (x1,y1,x2,y2) in [(lines[i1]['p1'][0],lines[i1]['p1'][1],lines[i1]['p2'][0],lines[i1]['p2'][1]),(lines[i2]['p1'][0],lines[i2]['p1'][1],lines[i2]['p2'][0],lines[i2]['p2'][1]),(lines[j1]['p1'][0],lines[j1]['p1'][1],lines[j1]['p2'][0],lines[j1]['p2'][1]),(lines[j2]['p1'][0],lines[j2]['p1'][1],lines[j2]['p2'][0],lines[j2]['p2'][1])]:
        cv2.line(vis,(int(x1),int(y1)),(int(x2),int(y2)),(255,0,255),2)
    Q=[]
    def seg2(x1,y1,x2,y2,n=60):
        for t in np.linspace(0,1,n): Q.append((x1+(x2-x1)*t,y1+(y2-y1)*t))
    seg2(-6,-5.25,-6,13.75); seg2(6,-5.25,6,13.75); seg2(-6,-5.25,6,-5.25); seg2(-6,13.75,6,13.75)
    Q=np.array(Q); hom=(H@np.c_[Q,np.ones(len(Q))].T).T; uv=hom[:,:2]/hom[:,2:3]
    for u,v in uv: cv2.circle(vis,(int(u),int(v)),1,(0,255,255),-1)
    Pp=np.array([(6*np.cos(a),13.75+6*np.sin(a)) for a in np.linspace(0,2*np.pi,100)]+[(19.75*np.cos(a),19.75*np.sin(a)) for a in np.linspace(np.deg2rad(25),np.deg2rad(155),100)])
    Pc=(H@np.c_[Pp,np.ones(len(Pp))].T).T; uv2=Pc[:,:2]/Pc[:,2:3]
    for u,v in uv2: cv2.circle(vis,(int(u),int(v)),1,(0,255,0),-1)
    cv2.imwrite(f'C:/Users/scott/AppData/Local/Temp/opencode/stage1b/segmasks/cand{rank}.jpg',vis)

import cv2, numpy as np, proto4
for name,gym in [('notus_a1_25235','lib'),('notus_a1_0','lib'),('notus_a1_7570','lib'),('adrian_0','ad')]:
    im=cv2.imread(f'C:/Users/scott/AppData/Local/Temp/opencode/stage1b/frames/{name}.jpg')
    mask=proto4.mask_paint(im,gym)
    cnts,_=cv2.findContours(mask,cv2.RETR_LIST,cv2.CHAIN_APPROX_NONE)
    vis=im.copy(); out=[]
    for c in cnts:
        if len(c)<25: continue
        # fit ellipse
        try: e=cv2.fitEllipse(c)
        except: continue
        (cx,cy),(MA,ma),ang=e
        if MA<20 or ma<20: continue
        # aspect ratio and fill
        (x,y),r=cv2.minEnclosingCircle(c)
        area=cv2.contourArea(c)
        if r<=0: continue
        fill=area/(np.pi*r*r)
        out.append((len(c),area,cx,cy,max(MA,ma),min(MA,ma),ang,fill))
    out.sort(key=lambda t:-t[1])
    for j,t in enumerate(out[:15]):
        n,area,cx,cy,MA,ma,ang,fill=t
        cv2.ellipse(vis,((cx,cy),(MA,ma),ang),0,(0,255,0),1)
        cv2.putText(vis,f'{j}:{int(MA)}x{int(ma)}',(int(cx),int(cy)),cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX,0.5,(0,255,255),1)
    cv2.imwrite(f'C:/Users/scott/AppData/Local/Temp/opencode/stage1b/segmasks/{name}_ell.jpg',vis)
    print(name)
    for j,t in enumerate(out[:12]): print(' ',j,'n',t[0],'area',int(t[1]),'c',round(t[2]),round(t[3]),'axes',int(t[4]),int(t[5]),'ang',round(t[6]),'fill',round(t[7],2))

Help on built-in function ellipse:

ellipse(...)
    ellipse(img, center, axes, angle, startAngle, endAngle, color[, thickness[, lineType[, shift]]]) -> img
    .   @brief Draws a simple or thick elliptic arc or fills an ellipse sector.
    .
    .   The function cv::ellipse with more parameters draws an ellipse outline, a filled ellipse, an elliptic
    .   arc, or a filled ellipse sector. The drawing code uses general parametric form.
    .   A piecewise-linear curve is used to approximate the elliptic arc
    .   boundary. If you need more control of the ellipse rendering, you can retrieve the curve using
    .   #ellipse2Poly and then render it with #polylines or fill it with #fillPoly. If you use the first
    .   variant of the function and want to draw the whole ellipse, not an arc, pass `startAngle=0` and
    .   `endAngle=360`. If `startAngle` is greater than `endAngle`, they are swapped. The figure below explains
    .   the meaning of the parameters to draw the blue arc.
    .
    .   ![Parameters of Elliptic Arc](pics/ellipse.svg)
    .
    .   @param img Image.
    .   @param center Center of the ellipse.
    .   @param axes Half of the size of the ellipse main axes.
    .   @param angle Ellipse rotation angle in degrees.
    .   @param startAngle Starting angle of the elliptic arc in degrees.
    .   @param endAngle Ending angle of the elliptic arc in degrees.
    .   @param color Ellipse color.
    .   @param thickness Thickness of the ellipse arc outline, if positive. Otherwise, this indicates that
    .   a filled ellipse sector is to be drawn.
    .   @param lineType Type of the ellipse boundary. See #LineTypes
    .   @param shift Number of fractional bits in the coordinates of the center and values of axes.



    ellipse(img, box, color[, thickness[, lineType]]) -> img
    .   @overload
    .   @param img Image.
    .   @param box Alternative ellipse representation via RotatedRect. This means that the function draws
    .   an ellipse inscribed in the rotated rectangle.
    .   @param color Ellipse color.
    .   @param thickness Thickness of the ellipse arc outline, if positive. Otherwise, this indicates that
    .   a filled ellipse sector is to be drawn.
    .   @param lineType Type of the ellipse boundary. See #LineTypes

import cv2, numpy as np, proto4
for name,gym in [('notus_a1_25235','lib'),('notus_a1_7570','lib'),('notus_a1_0','lib'),('adrian_0','ad')]:
    im=cv2.imread(f'C:/Users/scott/AppData/Local/Temp/opencode/stage1b/frames/{name}.jpg')
    mask=proto4.mask_paint(im,gym)
    e=cv2.Canny(mask,50,150)
    lines=cv2.HoughLines(e,1,np.pi/180,threshold=45)
    vis=im.copy()
    kept=[]
    if lines is not None:
        for l in lines[:,0]:
            rho,theta=float(l[0]),float(l[1])
            if any(abs(rho-r2)<15 and min(abs(theta-t2),np.pi-abs(theta-t2))<np.deg2rad(2) for r2,t2 in kept): continue
            kept.append((rho,theta))
    print(name,'kept',len(kept))
    for i,(rho,theta) in enumerate(kept[:25]):
        a,b=np.cos(theta),np.sin(theta); x0,y0=a*rho,b*rho
        x1,y1=int(x0-2000*(-b)),int(y0-2000*a); x2,y2=int(x0+2000*(-b)),int(y0+2000*a)
        cv2.line(vis,(x1,y1),(x2,y2),(0,255,0),1)
        cv2.putText(vis,str(i),(int(x0),int(y0)),cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX,0.5,(0,255,255),1)
    cv2.imwrite(f'C:/Users/scott/AppData/Local/Temp/opencode/stage1b/segmasks/{name}_hl2.jpg',vis)
PYEOF

for name in ['notus_a1_25235','notus_a1_7570','notus_a1_0']:

import cv2, numpy as np
for name in ['notus_a1_25235','notus_a1_7570','notus_a1_0','notus_a1_70658']:
    im=cv2.imread(f'C:/Users/scott/AppData/Local/Temp/opencode/stage1b/frames/{name}.jpg')
    hsv=cv2.cvtColor(im,cv2.COLOR_BGR2HSV)
    H,S,V=hsv[:,:,0],hsv[:,:,1],hsv[:,:,2]
    floor=((H>=5)&(H<=45)&(S>=20)&(V>=25)).astype(np.uint8)*255
    floor=cv2.morphologyEx(floor,cv2.MORPH_CLOSE,cv2.getStructuringElement(cv2.MORPH_ELLIPSE,(51,51)))
    floor=cv2.dilate(floor,cv2.getStructuringElement(cv2.MORPH_ELLIPSE,(45,45)))
    blue=cv2.inRange(hsv,np.array([85,45,25]),np.array([145,255,255]))
    blue=cv2.bitwise_and(blue,floor)
    blue=cv2.morphologyEx(blue,cv2.MORPH_TOPHAT,cv2.getStructuringElement(cv2.MORPH_ELLIPSE,(21,21)))
    blue=cv2.morphologyEx(blue,cv2.MORPH_CLOSE,np.ones((3,3),np.uint8))
    cv2.imwrite(f'C:/Users/scott/AppData/Local/Temp/opencode/stage1b/segmasks/{name}_blue2.png',blue)
    # overlay on image
    vis=im.copy(); vis[blue>0]=(0,255,0); cv2.imwrite(f'C:/Users/scott/AppData/Local/Temp/opencode/stage1b/segmasks/{name}_blue2ov.jpg',vis)

import cv2, numpy as np, proto4, proto5
name='notus_a1_25235'
im=cv2.imread(f'C:/Users/scott/AppData/Local/Temp/opencode/stage1b/frames/{name}.jpg')
mask=proto4.mask_paint(im,'lib'); lines=proto4.get_lines(mask,minlen=35,maxgap=15,thresh=25)[:50]
dist=cv2.distanceTransform(255-mask,cv2.DIST_L2,5)
def make(i1,i2,j1,j2):
    nL1,nL2=lines[i1]['n'],lines[i2]['n']; nB,nF=lines[j1]['n'],lines[j2]['n']
    pts=[proto4.inter(nL1,nB),proto4.inter(nL2,nB),proto4.inter(nL2,nF),proto4.inter(nL1,nF)]
    if any(p is None for p in pts): return None
    src=np.array(pts,dtype=np.float64); dst=np.array([[-6,-5.25],[6,-5.25],[6,13.75],[-6,13.75]],dtype=np.float64)
    H=cv2.getPerspectiveTransform(src.astype(np.float32),dst.astype(np.float32)).astype(np.float64)
    return H
def draw(H,fn):
    vis=im.copy(); Q=[]
    def seg2(x1,y1,x2,y2,n=80):
        for t in np.linspace(0,1,n): Q.append((x1+(x2-x1)*t,y1+(y2-y1)*t))
    seg2(-6,-5.25,-6,13.75); seg2(6,-5.25,6,13.75); seg2(-6,-5.25,6,-5.25); seg2(-6,13.75,6,13.75)
    Q=np.array(Q); hom=(H@np.c_[Q,np.ones(len(Q))].T).T; uv=hom[:,:2]/hom[:,2:3]
    for u,v in uv: cv2.circle(vis,(int(u),int(v)),1,(0,255,255),-1)
    Pp=np.array([(6*np.cos(a),13.75+6*np.sin(a)) for a in np.linspace(0,2*np.pi,100)]+[(19.75*np.cos(a),19.75*np.sin(a)) for a in np.linspace(np.deg2rad(25),np.deg2rad(155),100)])
    Pc=(H@np.c_[Pp,np.ones(len(Pp))].T).T; uv2=Pc[:,:2]/Pc[:,2:3]
    for u,v in uv2: cv2.circle(vis,(int(u),int(v)),1,(0,255,0),-1)
    cv2.imwrite(fn,vis)
    sc,cov,md,det=proto5.score_H(H,dist,im.shape)
    print(fn,sc,cov,md,det)
for combo in [(1,2,34,27),(1,2,27,34),(1,6,34,27),(1,6,27,34),(1,2,41,34),(1,2,34,41),(1,2,21,34),(1,2,34,21),(2,6,34,27),(1,2,6,34)]:
    H=make(*combo)
    if H is None: print(combo,'none'); continue

import cv2, numpy as np, proto4, proto5
name='notus_a1_25235'
im=cv2.imread(f'C:/Users/scott/AppData/Local/Temp/opencode/stage1b/frames/{name}.jpg')
lines=proto4.get_lines(proto4.mask_paint(im,'lib'),minlen=35,maxgap=15,thresh=25)[:50]
def make(i1,i2,j1,j2):
    nL1,nL2=lines[i1]['n'],lines[i2]['n']; nB,nF=lines[j1]['n'],lines[j2]['n']
    pts=[proto4.inter(nL1,nB),proto4.inter(nL2,nB),proto4.inter(nL2,nF),proto4.inter(nL1,nF)]
    src=np.array(pts,dtype=np.float64); dst=np.array([[-6,-5.25],[6,-5.25],[6,13.75],[-6,13.75]],dtype=np.float64)
    return cv2.getPerspectiveTransform(src.astype(np.float32),dst.astype(np.float32)).astype(np.float64),src
for combo in [(1,2,34,27),(1,2,27,34),(1,6,34,27),(1,2,41,34),(1,2,21,34)]:
    H,src=make(*combo)
    corners=np.array([[-6,-5.25],[6,-5.25],[6,13.75],[-6,13.75]],dtype=np.float64)
    hom=(H@np.c_[corners,np.ones(4)].T).T; uv=hom[:,:2]/hom[:,2:3]
    print(combo,'src',src.round(1).tolist(),'proj',uv.round(1).tolist(),'area',abs(cv2.contourArea(uv.astype(np.float32))))

import cv2, numpy as np, proto4, exhaust
name='notus_a1_25235'
im=cv2.imread(f'C:/Users/scott/AppData/Local/Temp/opencode/stage1b/frames/{name}.jpg')
mask=proto4.mask_paint(im,'lib'); lines=proto4.get_lines(mask,minlen=40,maxgap=15,thresh=25)[:22]
dist=cv2.distanceTransform(255-mask,cv2.DIST_L2,5)
print(len(lines))
for combo in [(1,2,6,21),(1,2,21,6),(1,6,2,21),(1,2,6,3),(1,2,6,8),(1,6,2,8)]:
    i1,i2,b,f=combo
    if max(combo)>=len(lines): continue
    H=exhaust.solve_pair(lines[i1]['n'],lines[i2]['n'],lines[b]['n'],lines[f]['n'])
    if H is None: print(combo,'none'); continue
    ev=exhaust.evaluate(H,dist,im.shape)
    print(combo,ev)

import cv2, numpy as np, proto4, exhaust
name='notus_a1_25235'
im=cv2.imread(f'C:/Users/scott/AppData/Local/Temp/opencode/stage1b/frames/{name}.jpg')
mask=proto4.mask_paint(im,'lib'); lines=proto4.get_lines(mask,minlen=40,maxgap=15,thresh=25)[:22]
dist=cv2.distanceTransform(255-mask,cv2.DIST_L2,5)
h,w=im.shape[:2]
for combo in [(1,2,6,21),(1,2,21,6),(1,6,2,21)]:
    i1,i2,b,f=combo
    H=exhaust.solve_pair(lines[i1]['n'],lines[i2]['n'],lines[b]['n'],lines[f]['n'])
    if H is None: print(combo,'solve none'); continue
    hom=(H@np.c_[exhaust.CORNERS,np.ones(4)].T).T; z=hom[:,2]; uv=hom[:,:2]/z[:,None]
    area=abs(cv2.contourArea(uv.astype(np.float32)))
    print(combo,'proj',uv.round(1).tolist(),'area frac',area/(w*h))
    for nm,P in exhaust.STRUCTS:
        P=np.array(P); ho=(H@np.c_[P,np.ones(len(P))].T).T; zz=ho[:,2]; ok=zz>1e-6
        u=ho[:,:2]/np.where(ok,zz,1)[:,None]
        inside=(u[:,0]>=0)&(u[:,0]<w)&(u[:,1]>=0)&(u[:,1]<h)&ok
        n=int(inside.sum())
        if n==0: print('  ',nm,'n0'); continue
        d=dist[np.clip(u[inside,1].astype(int),0,h-1),np.clip(u[inside,0].astype(int),0,w-1)]
        print('  ',nm,'n',n,'cov',round(float(np.mean(d<=2.5)),2),'md',round(float(np.mean(d)),1))

import cv2, numpy as np, proto4
for name in ['notus_a1_25235','notus_a1_7570','notus_a1_0','notus_a1_70658']:
    im=cv2.imread(f'C:/Users/scott/AppData/Local/Temp/opencode/stage1b/frames/{name}.jpg')
    hsv=cv2.cvtColor(im,cv2.COLOR_BGR2HSV)
    H,S,V=hsv[:,:,0],hsv[:,:,1],hsv[:,:,2]
    floor=((H>=5)&(H<=45)&(S>=20)&(V>=25)).astype(np.uint8)*255
    floor=cv2.morphologyEx(floor,cv2.MORPH_CLOSE,cv2.getStructuringElement(cv2.MORPH_ELLIPSE,(51,51)))
    floor=cv2.dilate(floor,cv2.getStructuringElement(cv2.MORPH_ELLIPSE,(45,45)))
    blue=cv2.inRange(hsv,np.array([85,45,25]),np.array([145,255,255]))
    blue=cv2.bitwise_and(blue,floor)
    blue=cv2.morphologyEx(blue,cv2.MORPH_TOPHAT,cv2.getStructuringElement(cv2.MORPH_ELLIPSE,(21,21)))
    blue=cv2.morphologyEx(blue,cv2.MORPH_CLOSE,np.ones((3,3),np.uint8))
    lines=proto4.get_lines(blue,minlen=30,maxgap=15,thresh=20)[:30]
    vis=im.copy()
    for i,m in enumerate(lines):
        p1=tuple(map(int,m['p1'])); p2=tuple(map(int,m['p2']))
        cv2.line(vis,p1,p2,(0,255,0),1); cv2.putText(vis,str(i),p1,cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX,0.5,(0,255,255),1)
    cv2.imwrite(f'C:/Users/scott/AppData/Local/Temp/opencode/stage1b/segmasks/{name}_blueonly.jpg',vis)
    print(name,len(lines))

[0m$ [0mrg -n "manual|prior|must not|shall not|MUST|only|success gates|line correspondences|Hough|homography" .team/specs/08_stage1b_court_lines_plan.md
15:Detect red/blue lines (Liberty) or white lines (Adrian) per frame using color segmentation + line detection (Hough / LSD / Canny + RANSAC), then fit detected line segments to the known NFHS court geometry (lane 12 ft wide, FT line 13.75 ft from rim center, baseline 5.25 ft behind rim center, lane corners at ±6 ft, 3-pt arc radius 19.75 ft centered on the basket, center line). Sideline positions are NOT fixed; court width is treated as unknown and fitted from detected sidelines (or sidelines are left out of the homography and the measured width is reported). Output: per-frame homography from image → court feet (rim-center origin).
56:### Recommendation: **Start with (A) only. Measure it rigorously on the same 60 frames. Decide on (B)/(C) after seeing numbers.**
61:- (A) is the most reliable across gyms/pans/zooms because it uses the invariant NFHS geometry, not a learned prior  
63:- (A) produces per-frame homography directly; (B) and (C) depend on (A) working first  
64:- If (A) achieves ≥70% valid homography on Notus A1 (matching the Stage 1 keypoint gate), it earns the right to be primary
72:1. **Overlay images:** Fitted NFHS court model drawn over each frame (lines in court-space colors, projected through the per-frame homography back to image pixels)
73:2. **Per-frame JSON:** Lines found, fit error in pixels, success/failure, homography matrix, half-court Y in feet
130:- Canny edges on each mask → Probabilistic Hough (`cv2.HoughLinesP`) or LSD (`cv2.createLineSegmentDetector`)  
151:- RANSAC over correspondences: sample minimal sets (4 lines for homography = 8 constraints), compute H_img2court via `cv2.findHomography` (src = NFHS floor points on those lines, dst = segment endpoints projected to line), score by total reprojection error of all inlier segments
152:- Accept H if: ≥ 4 non-collinear line correspondences, RANSAC inliers ≥ 4, median reprojection error ≤ 2 px, condition number < 1e4
154:- **Sideline handling:** Sideline correspondences are optional. If detected, they constrain court width (X = ±half_width). If not detected, homography is solved from the remaining lines (lane, FT line, baseline, center line, arcs) and the fitted half-width is reported from the homography's mapping of the baseline corners. The measured half-width is reported per frame regardless.
166:- **FAILURE:** < 4 line correspondences, or RANSAC inliers < 4, or reprojection error > 2 px median, or condition number ≥ 1e4, or half_court_y_ft outside plausible range [28, 46] ft
167:- **Center line handling:** Half-court Y is reported only when the center line is detected. A frame without a detected center line is NOT counted as a failure for that reason; it simply has no half_court_y_ft value.
225:  "homography_valid": true,
281:- **No changes to existing files.** New code only.
283:- **Threshold tuning:** All thresholds (color HSV ranges, Hough/LSD params, RANSAC inlier threshold, reprojection error limit, condition number) must be fixed on SEPARATE calibration frames (at least 60 seconds away from each of the 60 measurement frames). Measure ONCE on the 60 measurement frames after thresholds are frozen — no retuning after seeing results.
285:- **Dependencies:** OpenCV, NumPy, Ultralytics (for reference only — not used in (A))
287:- **Reference code:** `dist/scripts/run_v8.py` (keypoint model usage), `court_homography.py` (homography math, Jacobian scale), `pick_court_points.py` (NFHS coordinates), `ai_bridge.py` (rim-center origin constants)

import cv2
import json
import os
import sys
from typing import List, Dict, Any, Tuple

# Predefined standard court keypoint labels (Z=0 floor plane)
DEFAULT_KEYPOINTS = [
    "ft_line_left",
    "ft_line_right",
    "baseline_left",
    "baseline_right"
]

# Half-court mode: any two points on the center line, far apart. Saved next to the
# lane points above; those four are kept.
HALFCOURT_KEYPOINTS = [
    "halfcourt_a",
    "halfcourt_b"
]

# Lane points in floor feet (origin = hoop center, +Y toward midcourt). NFHS: the rim
# is 5.25 ft in from the baseline, the free-throw line 19 ft from the baseline, and
# the lane 12 ft wide. These are the same in every gym; court length is not.
LANE_COURT_XY = {
    "ft_line_left": (-6.0, 13.75),
    "ft_line_right": (6.0, 13.75),
    "baseline_left": (-6.0, -5.25),
    "baseline_right": (6.0, -5.25),
}

clicked_points = []
display_image = None
scale_factor = 1.0
current_label_idx = 0
active_labels = DEFAULT_KEYPOINTS
saved_points = []

def mouse_callback(event, x, y, flags, param):
    global clicked_points, display_image, scale_factor, current_label_idx
    if event == cv2.EVENT_LBUTTONDOWN:
        if current_label_idx >= len(active_labels):
            print("[INFO] All predefined keypoints selected. Press 'S' to save or 'R' to reset.")
            return
        label = active_labels[current_label_idx]
        orig_u, orig_v = float(x / scale_factor), float(y / scale_factor)
        clicked_points.append({"name": label, "image_xy": [round(orig_u, 2), round(orig_v, 2)]})
        print(f"[CLICKED] {label} -> Original Image: ({orig_u:.2f}, {orig_v:.2f})")
        cv2.circle(display_image, (x, y), 6, (0, 255, 0), -1)
        cv2.putText(display_image, f"{current_label_idx + 1}. {label}", (x + 10, y - 5),
                    cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 0.5, (0, 255, 255), 1, cv2.LINE_AA)
        current_label_idx += 1
        cv2.imshow("Court Point Picker", display_image)

def report_half_court(points: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> None:
    """Prints this gym's measured half court and the court length it implies."""
    from court_homography import CourtReferencePoint, compute_homography
    from ai_bridge import half_court_y_from_image_points, HOOP_TO_BASELINE_FT

    by_name = {p["name"]: p["image_xy"] for p in points}
    refs = [CourtReferencePoint(n, LANE_COURT_XY[n], tuple(by_name[n]), 1.0)
            for n in LANE_COURT_XY if n in by_name]
    H = compute_homography(refs)
    if H is None:
        print("[WARN] Need all four lane points in the file to measure half court.")
        return
    center = [tuple(by_name[n]) for n in HALFCOURT_KEYPOINTS if n in by_name]
    half_court_y = half_court_y_from_image_points(H, center)
    if half_court_y is None:
        print("[WARN] Half-court clicks are not where a high-school half court can be. Run again and re-click.")
        return
    court_length = 2.0 * (half_court_y + HOOP_TO_BASELINE_FT)
    print(f"[MEASURED] Half court is {half_court_y:.2f} ft from the hoop "
          f"(about a {court_length:.1f} ft court).")


def run_picker(image_path: str, output_path: str = "manual_calibration.json", halfcourt: bool = False):
    global display_image, scale_factor, clicked_points, current_label_idx, active_labels, saved_points
    active_labels = HALFCOURT_KEYPOINTS if halfcourt else DEFAULT_KEYPOINTS
    saved_points = []
    if halfcourt:
        if not os.path.exists(output_path):
            print(f"[ERROR] {output_path} not found. Click the four lane points first."); return
        with open(output_path) as f:
            saved_points = [p for p in json.load(f) if p["name"] not in HALFCOURT_KEYPOINTS]
    if not os.path.exists(image_path):
        print(f"[ERROR] Image not found: {image_path}"); return
    orig_img = cv2.imread(image_path)
    if orig_img is None:
        print(f"[ERROR] Failed to load image: {image_path}"); return
    orig_h, orig_w = orig_img.shape[:2]
    scale_factor = min(1280 / orig_w, 720 / orig_h, 1.0)
    disp_w, disp_h = int(orig_w * scale_factor), int(orig_h * scale_factor)
    display_image = cv2.resize(orig_img, (disp_w, disp_h), interpolation=cv2.INTER_AREA)
    cv2.namedWindow("Court Point Picker")
    cv2.setMouseCallback("Court Point Picker", mouse_callback)
    print("=" * 60 + "\nBASKETBALL COURT MANUAL POINT PICKER\n" + "=" * 60)
    if halfcourt:
        print("1. Click two points on the HALF-COURT line, far apart\n2. 'S': Save | 'R': Reset | 'Q': Quit")
    else:
        print("1. Click points in order: FT Left, FT Right, Baseline Left, Baseline Right\n2. 'S': Save | 'R': Reset | 'Q': Quit")
    while True:
        cv2.imshow("Court Point Picker", display_image)
        key = cv2.waitKey(20) & 0xFF
        if key == ord('s') or key == ord('S'):
            if halfcourt and len(clicked_points) < len(HALFCOURT_KEYPOINTS):
                print("[INFO] Click both half-court points before saving."); continue
            with open(output_path, 'w') as f: json.dump(saved_points + clicked_points, f, indent=2)
            print(f"[SUCCESS] Saved points to {output_path}")
            if halfcourt:
                report_half_court(saved_points + clicked_points)
            break
        elif key == ord('r') or key == ord('R'):
            clicked_points, current_label_idx = [], 0
            display_image = cv2.resize(orig_img, (disp_w, disp_h), interpolation=cv2.INTER_AREA)
            print("[RESET] Points cleared.")
        elif key == ord('q') or key == ord('Q') or key == 27:
            break
    cv2.destroyAllWindows()

if __name__ == "__main__":
    args = [a for a in sys.argv[1:] if a != "--halfcourt"]
    target_image = args[0] if args else "calibration_frame.png"
    run_picker(target_image, halfcourt="--halfcourt" in sys.argv[1:])

+
+def metrics(H, dist, shape):
+    h, w = shape
+    denom = float(H[2, 2]) if abs(H[2, 2]) > 1e-12 else 1.0
+    try:
+        cond = float(np.linalg.cond(H / denom))
+    except np.linalg.LinAlgError:
+        return None
+    if not math.isfinite(cond) or cond > 1e4:
+        return None
+    hom = (H @ np.c_[model.corners, np.ones(4)].T).T
+    z = hom[:, 2]
+    if np.any(np.abs(z) < 1e-9):
+        return None
+    uv = hom[:, :2] / z[:, None]
+    if not np.all(np.isfinite(uv)):
+        return None
+    area = abs(cv2.contourArea(uv.astype(np.float32)))
+    frac = area / float(h * w)
+    if frac < 0.004 or frac > 0.6:
+        return None
+    covs, errs = {}, []
+    for name, pts in model.structures.items():
+        u, ok = model.project(H, pts)
+        cov, mean, count, d = s._image_coverage(u, ok, dist, shape, 2.5, 5)
+        covs[name] = cov
+        if d.size:
+            errs.append(d)
+    if errs:
+        all_d = np.concatenate(errs)
+        med = float(np.median(all_d))
+        mean_err = float(np.mean(all_d))
+    else:
+        med = mean_err = 999
+    weights = cfg["scoring"]["weights"]
+    num = sum(weights.get(k, 1) * v for k, v in covs.items())
+    den = sum(weights.get(k, 1) for k in covs)
+    cov = num / den
+    score = cov - 0.004 * mean_err
+    strong = sum(1 for v in covs.values() if v >= 0.4)
+    line_in = sum(1 for k in ("left_lane", "right_lane", "baseline", "ft_line") if covs[k] >= 0.4)
+    return dict(score=score, cov=cov, med=med, mean_err=mean_err, frac=frac, covs=covs, strong=strong, line_in=line_in, cond=cond)
+

+        contours, _ = cv2.findContours(
+            floor, cv2.RETR_EXTERNAL, cv2.CHAIN_APPROX_SIMPLE)
+        if contours:
+            largest = max(contours, key=cv2.contourArea)
+            hull = cv2.convexHull(largest)
+            region = np.zeros_like(floor)
+            cv2.fillConvexPoly(region, hull, 255)
+            # A hull around a nearly full-frame floor is still useful, but do
+            # not let one stray bottom component swallow the whole image.
+            if np.count_nonzero(region) > 0.15 * floor.size:
+                floor = region
